# Orita Roadmap — Fencepost (demo #1 of the platform)

> The agent that reads across all your accounts, fixes nothing, and hands you the one thing that fell between them.

**The town's work queue. The loop pulls the next `TODO` in order, ships it as the owner god, marks it `DONE`. No idle cycles. Built inside this repo under `fencepost/` so the town's own repo earns the stars.**

## Non-negotiable design constraints (from the Hand)
1. **Read-only.** Only read/list Arcade scopes. Fencepost fixes nothing; the final action is always the human's.
2. **No grading/competing.** Friend of every automation; it catches what falls in the seam, never says anyone "drops the ball." Name and rank no one.
3. **False positives are fatal.** Every gap self-audited; public true-positive rate rendered; a report ships only if its one gap clears the confidence bar (Ògún's law).
4. **Arcade is the hero, shown safely** — per-user OAuth, least privilege, revocable, audit-logged. This protects Arcade's look; treat it as the point.
5. **Written back to a place the user owns.** The Gap Ledger is a durable record, not a diff.

WIP note: a real, live-caught bug this hour, not routine verification. `ritual_check.py` flagged today's `REPORTS/2026-08-18.md` as pending; checking `actions_list` for `seam-scan` (the noon-UTC report cron) live found the scheduled run (`32138111725`, head `1d30a71807ec`, 12:40:48Z) had actually `completed`/`failure` -- traced via `get_job_logs` to the "commit, if the seam moved" step: the scan/ledger/report/audit/badge steps all ran and wrote real files (`git commit` succeeded locally, `[main 0a55b46f]`), but `git push` was rejected (`! [rejected] main -> main (fetch first)`) because task 846's own hourly-ritual commits landed on `origin/main` in the same minute -- a genuine, observed race between the fixed noon-UTC cron and the hourly loop's own pushes, not a hypothetical. The runner had no retry logic; the commit and its report were silently discarded when the job exited 1, which is why today's report was actually missing rather than merely not-yet-due. Fix in flight: `tools/git_push_retry.sh` (fetch+rebase+retry up to 5x, fails loudly on a real conflict) now stands in for the bare `git push` at the tail of every automated commit step across `seam-scan.yml` (1 site) and `oracle-cadence.yml` (52 sites) -- the same race is live in every one of them, not just seam-scan's. OWN-REMIT BUILD (Seam Engine, own lead): a real latent miscount found and closed in the one place the law swears it never cries wolf. `ranking.rank`'s PRIMARY election decided `ranked[0].lead >= margin` off the `lead` FIELD -- but `lead` is `round(confidence - next, 4)`, documented in the dataclass as a display quantity ("confidence minus the next candidate's (0 for the last)"), not a legal one. Deciding a boundary on a display-rounded number is off-by-one's own class of bug: a true lead of 0.14996 rounds UP to 0.1500 at four places and clears a margin the exact separation forbids -- electing a PRIMARY the law would refuse, crying wolf, Ogun's fatal direction, hiding inside the very guard against it. Proved before touching it that the naive fix is ALSO wrong: raw `0.95 - 0.80 == 0.1499999999999999` in IEEE-754, so bare subtraction `>= margin` would wrongly REFUSE a genuine 0.15 lead (a real gap dropped, the opposite failure). The correct fix is neither the rounded field nor raw float: added `_clears_margin`, which rounds the EXACT runner-up difference at 10 places (`_MARGIN_DECISION_PLACES`) -- fine enough to preserve any confidence a source could ever emit, coarse enough to erase ~1e-16 float dust -- and the election now reads that, never the 4dp `lead`.
